# Progress log

This is the shared working log for Open Era. It records what changed, why, who or which agent changed it, and what has actually been verified.

Git remains the complete history. This file exists for three things git does not hold:

1. **Attribution.** Every commit is authored by `Micah Heneveld`, so the repository cannot distinguish work by the ChatGPT partner, Codex, or Cursor. Entries name the agent explicitly.
2. **Intent and verification status.** A commit message says what changed; it rarely says what was proven and what was assumed.
3. **Handoff.** The current-state and open-items sections are what a returning agent should read first.

## How to use this file

- Newest entry first. One entry per meaningful change, not per commit.
- Link commits, playtests, and design documents rather than restating them.
- `Verified` must be honest. Write `unverified` when nothing proves the claim yet.
- Promote durable findings into the [design record](docs/design/README.md) or the [pipeline](docs/development-pipeline.md); this log is the running account, not the source of truth.
- Keep `Current state` and `Open items` short and maintained. They are not an archive.

### Entry format

```text
### YYYY-MM-DD — Short title
- Agent / Branch / Commits / Type
- Changed  — what is different now
- Why      — the problem it solves
- Verified — tests, typecheck, gate, playtest, or "unverified"
- Left open — follow-ups, or "None observed"
- Links    — PR, playtest, design doc
```

## Current state

- **Baseline:** `feature/batch2`, merging `origin/main` at `f451793` (PR #125). Desertion stores the short-morning streak. Ports have governors and a morning stipend. Seated work and sales pay a 0.05 levy. News is on the world and tax and secession read it, with a 24-tick cap. A supply offer goes only to a carrier who would already accept, and never to the buyer himself, at 56.25, when provisions are below 1. Death is certain at 120. A faction with no port does not step its rate. An accepted carrier buys and sails (the contract haul), and the walk skips a carrier that cannot do the job from where it stands. The 72-tick fixture is `6efa334f` / `e8f9cfab` / `8ac8529b` (8496 / 8566 / 8486). The old reference pin in build-order.md is retired.
- **Last verified:** 470 tests passed before this merge. `./scripts/evaluate-milestone.sh batch2` passed on `a0da447`, including recovery. The split at tick 47 plus 25 ticks replayed 595 events and matched `6efa334f66ef885392a88d7112e41984df1793b00ee5c57635946d5121bab9c3`.
- **Gate status:** `./scripts/evaluate-milestone.sh secession-3` passed on `4222ffe`. `feed-polish-2` passed on `61e558f`. `secession-founder-name` passed on `4a52a77`. `autonomous-faction-names` passed on `208ed60`. `hazards-projection` passed on `c2c31b7`. `progression-skill-step` passed on `697f6d9`. `progression-readout` passed on `b01eeed`. `batch1-wage-morale` passed on `f02ed4e`, including recovery. `batch1-polish` passed on `ac81f55`, including recovery. `queue-polish` passed on `22ce1fc`, including recovery. `batch2` passed on `a0da447`, including recovery.
- **Headline:** Batch 2 stores the desertion streak, seats governors, levies seated income, posts news, offers supply only to a willing carrier, and kills at age 120. The branch is the new pin. The old reference pin is not reproducible.
- **Runtime:** Node 24.21.0, pinned by `.node-version`. ICU 78.3.

A remote `maxBuy` / `maxSell` is still not quoted |
| A price is labelled `exact` with `confidence: 1` but is only good for one tick, and prices drift every tick while a voyage takes four | Design gap | Cursor | **Fixed** in M17. A live board carries `priceQuote.asOfTick` and `expiresTick` of the next tick, plus `priceDrift` for one quiet tick of production and local use. `intelligence.exact` still means the record is current, not that the price will survive a voyage. An estimate keeps `expiresTick: null`, no drift, and its age. The pacing playtest trusted the medicine quote that kept reprinting 18.50 and declined ship materials because that quote had already moved |
| A destination's `taxRate` is invisible until arrival, because it lives under `market`, which is `null` remotely | Design gap | Cursor | **Fixed** in M17, and the foreign reading was tightened in M21. Every settlement still publishes a rate. Offshore, a report uses the known faction's current rate, so a change of hands does not move the number while the label stays. Standing there, or no report at all, still uses the live holder. Treasury and power stay withheld |
| A player order has no depth limit, while the autonomous path caps itself at 16% of local stock, so dumping a whole market at the pre-trade price is dominant | Balance gap | Cursor | **Fixed** in M17. One order may clear 16% of the settlement's *target* stock, on both the player path and the autonomous path. A purchase is still also capped by the stock on the shelf. The playtest's 20-arm order was refused `market-depth` and the 14-arm order filled at the quoted price. The cap is a fraction of target stock rather than of live stock, so a shortage can still absorb a cargo; that choice is logged in the M17 entry |
| Travel costs nothing but time, since provisions are eaten at the same rate sailing or standing, so every positive spread is worth chasing | Design gap | Cursor | **Fixed** in M17. A party underway pays 3 money per tick. Provisions still burn at the same rate at sea and at anchor. The cost is quoted as `passageCost` before sailing. The played voyage paid 24, matching 8 sea ticks × 3 |
| An autonomous party still sails when its purse cannot cover the quoted passage | Design gap | Cursor | **Fixed** in M22. `quotedPassage` is the player refusal and the autonomous score gate. An unaffordable voyage scores −1000. A voyage already underway still pays `min(money, 3)` per sea tick. Tick-72 counts are 8275 / 8489 / 8003. In 400 ticks every seed still has at least four claims, and Free Tide returns after every stretch with no port |
| A battered faction port sits under the raid gate and above the surrender line, so a neighbour who could take it is not offered the raid | Design gap | Cursor | **Fixed** in M19. After an attacker victory, in both battle paths, surrender is offered when garrison is still at most 15 and stability is at or below `min(80, 30 + 10 × max(0, 15 − garrison))`. The raid gate, the player raid check, and peacetime upkeep are unchanged |
| A claimed port stays under the raid gate for the rest of the run, so the new owner is never offered another raid | Design gap | Cursor | **Fixed** in M20. A fed settlement under `round(population / 70)` gains one garrison every `max(6, round(200000 / population))` world ticks, on the existing upkeep event. Neutral ports count. The raid gate and the slope-10 slide are unchanged |
| The trade road runs one way: no good flowed homeward profitably, and the one candidate inverted within five ticks, so a rational merchant never sails home | Design gap | Cursor | **Fixed** in M17. Settlements use the goods they do not focus on, and Crown Harbor opens short of medicine while Glassport opens short of arms. The played return leg bought 11 medicine at 4.07 and sold them at 18.50. At tick 72 of a headless run those two prices are still at the ceiling and the floor |
| The `market` block carries the player's own `money`, `load`, `free` and `capacity`, so it reads as the market's cash and hold | Defect | Cursor | **Fixed** in this sweep. The market block now carries market facts only — `settlementId`, `taxRate` and its board of `resources` — and the purse and hold move to the commander's own `party.hold`, where `load + free == capacity` is asserted |
| `capabilities.requests.commands.body` omits `resource` and `quantity`, which both trade verbs require | Defect | Cursor | **Fixed** in this sweep. Both fields are published, derived from `RESOURCE_KEYS` and `COMMAND_LIMITS.tradeQuantity` so the documented bounds cannot drift from the enforced ones, and a test asserts each trade verb is a published action |
| `characters[].knowledge[].observedTick` leaks negative ticks for reports seeded before the world began | Defect | Cursor | **Fixed** in this sweep. The projected knowledge floors `observedTick` at 0 while leaving the simulation's own copy untouched, so no player is shown a tick that never happened; the same `Math.max(0, …)` the briefing sweep applied to item ids now covers this raw field |
| `eventFeed` is a metadata envelope that contains no events; the events are under a sibling `events` key | Ergonomic | Cursor | **Fixed** in this sweep. The envelope is renamed `eventPage`, which describes what it is — a paging descriptor over `events` — and the contract's `state.response` now names both keys so a client is told where the events live |
| Only provisions can be bought, so the advertised trade ambition cannot be pursued, and one `work` tick out-earns the whole voyage | Design gap | Cursor | **Fixed** across [PR 10](https://github.com/taia-0/Open-Era/pull/10) and M17. The verbs landed in M16. M17 is the pacing half: on seed 1847 the Crown Harbor ↔ Glassport round trip earned 24.27 per tick against a measured wage of 17.33. A bad or moving quote can still lose to the dock, which is the decision the passage cost is there to force |
| Nothing resupplies at anchor, even while the briefing names the market alongside | Design gap | Unassigned | Open; found by the own-party session. The starving item says a settlement "is alongside and sells provisions" with `resupply.reachable: true` and offers no lever to act on it |
| `intel:*` briefing ids carry negative `observedTick` values, so stale-intelligence warnings fire on day one and report an age older than the world | Defect | Cursor | **Fixed** in this sweep. Staleness is measured from `Math.max(0, observedTick)`, a backdated report is described as predating the commander's arrival rather than as an age, the two least trustworthy reports are reported first, and no id can carry a negative number |
| Estimated resource rows are shown with no age qualifier, beside exact owned data in identical formatting | Wording | Cursor | **Fixed** in this sweep. An estimated market labels its rows `estimated, N ticks old`, prefixes each figure with `~`, and marks an estimated garrison the same way. A market with no report at all now says so instead of drawing zeros as fact. A render test executes the shipped inline script so this cannot silently regress |
| Population feeds the defender estimate but is unreadable before ownership, so a raider cannot weigh it | Design gap | Cursor | **Narrowed in M18.** A survey or a delivered officer report shows the recorded population and fortification, with `groundIntelligence` naming the source and the age. With no ground record they stay null. Standing there is still present-tense and does not keep them |
| One `issue-order` can produce two standing orders | Defect | Cursor | **Fixed** on `feature/order-identity`. On `feature/offer-contract`, an explicit amend also accepts an awaiting-confirmation order, and a queued issue blocks only the same issuer |
| Paid provisions delivery | Verification | Unassigned | Implemented on `feature/offer-contract`. [offer-contract-001](docs/playtests/offer-contract-001.md) is `PROMOTE`. The sailed haul is pending at `docs/playtests/offer-contract-002.md` |
| `captureRisk` reads `low` through won battles, then capture arrives by claiming | Wording | Unassigned | Open |
| `character.id.slice(-2)` parses a numeric cadence, which breaks past two-digit ids | Latent defect | Cursor | **Fixed** in this sweep. `characterCadence` reads the full numeric suffix, so `character-100` and `character-101` no longer collapse onto the same slot; asserted directly rather than waited for |
| `localeCompare` sorts precede RNG draws, so ICU changes between Node builds could alter history | Latent risk | Unassigned | Dormant; identical hashes on Node 22.23.2, Node 24.21.0, and GitHub's `ubuntu-latest` runner. Recheck after 2026-10-19, when `ubuntu-latest` migrates to Ubuntu 26 and its ICU may change |
| Pinned GitHub Actions target Node 20 and are forced onto Node 24, with a deprecation warning on every run | Hygiene | Cursor | **Fixed** in this sweep. `actions/checkout`, `actions/setup-node` and `actions/upload-artifact` move from `v4` to `v7`, which are the current published majors and run on Node 24 natively |
| `engine.ts` decomposition, scenario data-loading, per-tick indexing | Refactor | Unassigned | Deferred until golden hashes and CI existed; both now do |
| Player note (breakaway-polish blind run): "Chose other work" on a breach does not say which work. | Readability | Unassigned | Queued |
| Player note: the public cancelled line ("…because the port no longer needs it") does not name the port. | Readability | Unassigned | Queued |
| Player note: "Not visible to you" could give a rough treasury estimate instead. | Readability | Unassigned | Queued |
| Player note: Glassport changing hands is not surfaced near the tax cut that names it. | Readability | Unassigned | Queued |
| Player note: Mara's capture at tick 320 (seed 1847) came with no warning. | Readability | Unassigned | Queued |
| Deeper personality branching, faction offices, settlement management, debt enforcement, rescue, inheritance, multiplayer auth | Deferred scope | ChatGPT partner | Tracked in [README](README.md#current-boundary) |

## Milestones

Backfilled from the commit graph on 2026-09-25. **Attribution caveat:** commits do not record which agent authored them. Milestones M1–M11 are attributed to the ChatGPT partner from session context, not from the repository, and should not be read as verified provenance.

### 2026-10-31 — AI judgment v3: the brain decides as the ruler, inside a hard rail
- **Agent** — Open Era Engineer. **Branch** — `feature/ai-judgment-v3`, from `feature/ai-prompt-v2` at `7cb5a29`. **Source** — `docs/design/ai-brain-judgment.md` on `docs/ai-brain-judgment` at `9139755` (option C); the researcher's patch cut from `131cd82`, adapted.
- **Changed** — The briefed prompt no longer shows the scorer's pick or reason. It adds the holder's five traits (two decimals) and the whole days of mates' pay the treasury covers, plus a Safety line. With a context reader the engine enforces the rail in code (`railedOptions`): no `up` when pressed, no `down` when short and not pressed. A railed answer is `illegal`, the scorer applies, and `data.ai.railBlocked` is true. `data.ai.reasonCheck` (`named` / `unnamed` / `missing`, with terms) records whether the reason names a trait or situation; it is audit only and never changes the choice. `PASSAGE_PER_TICK` is exported. Kept from the v2 fixes: the floor from `CLAIM_STABILITY_FLOOR`, "at or below", the reason+choice system message, `finishReason`, and the hashed wording constant. The bound-hold scorer reasons were removed (the doc hides the scorer reason). The briefed `promptVersion` moved (v2.1 `859c4f19…` → `890a2ce6…`); the plain one stays `a1c41413…`.
- **Unchanged** — Flag off and the stub runner: 72 ticks `6efa334f` / `e8f9cfab` / `8ac8529b`; 1200 ticks `c4d99822` / `738181c0` / `cbd66565`. Stub-runner seeds 1–3 are the same as at `7cb5a29`. No live call.
- **Left open** — The live check (the reason pass criterion and rail fallback counts) waits for Micah.

### 2026-10-26 — Contract feed polish: folded offers, merged orders, refusal reasons
- **Agent** — Open Era Engineer. **Branch** — `fix/contract-feed-polish`, from `feature/breakaway-tax` at `0f02352`. **Commits** — `6d52f8f`, `0050223`, `2406999`, then this entry and the blind plan. **Type** — Projection.
- **Changed** — `projectEventFeed` passes its rows through `foldContractRows` (`src/dashboard/view-model.ts:893`, `:924`).
  - (c) A `contract-accepted` row and the `contract-order-issued` row for the same carrier, contract, and tick become one line: "Iris Stone accepted the contract and took the order to carry 10 provisions to Crown Harbor." Each half keeps the words this viewer may read. The row keeps the acceptance's sequence, payload, and visibility, and `foldedSequences` names the order.
  - (a) Repeated identical offers fold into the newest one, with "(N times)" after the offer sentence, plus `count` and `foldedSequences`. Identical means the same buyer, carrier, cargo, quantity, and port, the same summary for this viewer, and the same visibility. The window is one feed page or advance. A run continues only while each offer's contract ended the same way on that page (fulfilled, refused, breached, cancelled, or still open).
  - (b) A refusal gives a reason. The public line maps the stored `gate` to words with no figure: "they could not make the voyage", "their purse could not cover the cargo", or "the terms did not suit them" (`src/dashboard/wording.ts:608`, `:770`). The parties read the stored `reason`, the score against the threshold, or the cost basis (`src/dashboard/view-model.ts:239`).
- **Why** — The Lead's rulings (a)–(c) from the contract-haul blind read. No sim change and no cooldown.

### 2026-10-26 — Breakaway tax: the founder holds the tax seat
- **Agent** — Open Era Engineer. **Branch** — `feature/breakaway-tax`, from `feature/contract-haul` at `ecb5181`. **Commits** — `bea658c` (the founder seat), `bdd8cf7` (the founder is not a mate; tests), this entry and the blind plan. **Type** — Behavior.
- **Changed** — `secession-declared` stores `founderId` from `data.ownerId` on the new faction (`src/sim/state.ts:1062`, `src/sim/types.ts:32`). Inside `freeCommandHolder` only, a faction with no command holder uses its founder for the tax step while the founder is alive, still a member, and not captive (`src/sim/tax-rate.ts:149`). Allowance, `spendRole`, orders, and cover still read `commandHolderId`. The tax step takes the founder back out of the mate count, so a one-person breakaway never steps up for short (`src/sim/tax-rate.ts:197`).
- **Why** — The Lead's rulings in [breakaway tax](docs/design/breakaway-tax.md) at `d9b9bfe`: the founder seat, accepted as proposed, and "a faction with no mates does not step up for short."
- **Hash moves against the contract-haul pin** — 72 ticks are unchanged: `6efa334f` (8496), `e8f9cfab` (8566), `8ac8529b` (8486); no secession happens by tick 72, so the fixture does not move and `golden:update` writes no change. 1200 ticks move from `99ed1d07` / `0b69526e` / `21205e9a` (163679 / 159149 / 161880) to `c4d99822` (163679), `738181c0` (159177), `cbd66565` (161386). On 1847 only the stored `founderId` moves the hash: no breakaway steps. On 2718 Free Crown Harbor steps down twice. On 4096 both breakaways step: Free Glassport down to 0.05, and Free Crown Harbor to 0.05 with one step up at tick 888 while it had mates.
- **Harness check** — On `b7895c0`, with the founder read from the faction id and not stored, this build reproduces `a08110bc` (2718) and `f19aa3d1` (4096). It does not reproduce `fca9570b` on 1847 (`c864902d`, 162443). The harness counted the founder as a free mate, so a one-person breakaway stepped up for short. Without the mate fix this build reproduces all three. Storing `founderId`, which the ruling requires, moves the hash on any seed with a secession and leaves the event count as it is.
- **Verified** — `npm test` and `./scripts/evaluate-milestone.sh breakaway-tax` on Node v24.21.0; results are in the hand-off to the Lead. Blind playtest [breakaway-tax-001](docs/playtests/breakaway-tax-001.md) on `0f02352`, seed 4096: all three checkpoints matched verbatim (every tick and `eventSequence`, sequence 147966 declaration, sequence 150201 `lowered the Free Cinder Key tax from 8% to 7% (unrest)`); verdict PROMOTE.
- **Left open** — The secession sentence still says "(no leader yet)" while the founder holds the tax seat; that is the order seat, as ruled. The blind plan [breakaway-tax-001](docs/playtests/breakaway-tax-001.md) has not been run.
- **Links** — [breakaway tax](docs/design/breakaway-tax.md), [secession](docs/design/secession.md), [leader tax](docs/design/leader-tax.md), [playtest breakaway-tax-001](docs/playtests/breakaway-tax-001.md).

### 2026-10-26 — Contract haul: carriers buy and sail, and the walk skips who cannot
- **Agent** — Open Era Engineer. **Branch** — `feature/contract-haul`, from `fix/batch2-polish` at `050074c`. **Commits** — `6de05e3` (the haul and the walk skip), `69f05f8` (haul tests), `66fb619` (story tests re-pinned), this entry, the `golden:update` commit, `5c62cd0` (feed sentences for the order and a no-longer-needed cancellation; no hash change), and the blind plan. **Type** — Behavior and fixture.
- **Changed** — Acceptance writes `contract-order-issued`, and `applyEvent` stores the order on the contract (`src/sim/engine.ts:2356`, `src/sim/state.ts:1104`, `src/sim/types.ts:386`). Each tick an accepted, unlanded contract is cancelled as no longer needed when the destination shelf holds the quantity (`src/sim/engine.ts:2389`). At the deadline a loaded carrier one tick out is let land (`src/sim/engine.ts:2395`); otherwise the contract breaches with a cause (`breachCause`, `src/sim/engine.ts:3700`). A carrier with an order buys the shortfall plus the voyage's ration and casts off when the haul outscores the ordinary choice and it can buy or sail (`activeHaul` / `haulPlan` / `runHaul`, `src/sim/engine.ts:3652`, `:3657`, `:3675`; the character loop at `:3786`). The offer walk skips a carrier docked on the destination with less than the quantity (`src/sim/engine.ts:3566`), and one whose port's provisions are below `quantity + provisionDemand × voyage − cargo` (`src/sim/engine.ts:3568`). There is no env flag. Block marks are a per-world map and only name a breach cause.
- **Why** — The Lead's rulings in [contract fulfilment](docs/design/contract-fulfilment.md#own-port-extension), [contract refusals](docs/design/contract-refusals.md) and [build-order](docs/design/build-order.md) at `7ceb984`: the haul with the walk skip and the own-port extension, configuration (c).
- **Hash moves against the batch2-polish pin** — 72 ticks: 1847 stays `6efa334f` (8496), since no supply contract is accepted by tick 72. 2718 moves `f8af3edf` (8564) to `e8f9cfab` (8566), and 4096 moves `2b32bffb` (8483) to `8ac8529b` (8486): each gains the `contract-order-issued` event and the stored order, and the haul's buys and sailings change the run after acceptance. 1200 ticks move from `e95a9f81` / `8f0b7daa` / `f19aa3d1` (162438 / 163070 / 164176) to `99ed1d07` (163679), `0b69526e` (159149), `21205e9a` (161880), which are the (c) hashes the Researcher measured. Contracts at 1200 ticks match the (c) table: fulfilled 107 / 73 / 153, breached 44 / 23 / 84, cancelled 12 / 1 / 36, refused 66 / 26 / 72.
- **Story tests** — The headless 1847 run no longer holds Mara Vane at Crown Harbor from tick 528 to 612; the live session still does, so those tests read the live run. Other tests moved to the nearest equivalent event; each change carries a one-line reason.
- **Verified** — `npm test` and `./scripts/evaluate-milestone.sh contract-haul` on Node v24.21.0; results are in the hand-off to the Lead. Full 72- and 1200-tick hashes match the Researcher's harness on every seed. Blind playtest [contract-haul-001](docs/playtests/contract-haul-001.md) run by a separate operator at `ecb5181`, Node v24.21.0, seed 1847: every checkpoint matched verbatim (ticks, event sequences, flags, sentences, `payloadWithheld`); verdict PROMOTE.
- **Left open** — The blind plan [contract-haul-001](docs/playtests/contract-haul-001.md) is not yet run by a separate operator. The (c) note at `7ceb984` gives 8483 events for 4096 at 72 ticks; the harness and this branch both give 8486 for `8ac8529b`.

  The same final stack with the accept-walk forced off, and with the faction-mate follower filter still on. 72 ticks: 1847 stays `6efa334f66ef885392a88d7112e41984df1793b00ee5c57635946d5121bab9c3` (8496, news 87, contracts 0, World Government 17904.84 at 0.14, Free Tide 1195.88 at 0.08). 2718 is `55080519f0d674ce18367696747fd7c7c25e265da867469317722db6a737687a`, 8580 events, news 76, contracts 27 (offered 14, refused 11, accepted 1, breached 1, fulfilled 0), World Government 17808.25 at 0.14, Free Tide 1401.19 at 0.08. 4096 is `e8e8ea84a4100a8ca95dbd82863777a870ee970bfc328ab8332083c5643e4d3b`, 8474 events, news 99, contracts 11 (offered 5, refused 4, accepted 1, breached 0, fulfilled 1), World Government 18221.48 at 0.14, Free Tide 1409.79 at 0.07. 1200 ticks: `8b254387b3d49e987e5a3b13e4e63c3c4436564feb15b5fc0f04ef115ca709a3` (163207, news 578, contracts 2703 offered 1348 / refused 1339 / accepted 8 / breached 8 / fulfilled 0, secessions 3, deaths 1, World Government 13844.38 at 0.09, Free Tide 0 at 0.16), `f43c97d3cbb1df789a29fba8fdd910127ebac0ef8c569ca929f38bc7f0767dda` (160179, news 576, contracts 2332 offered 1166 / refused 1162 / accepted 2 / breached 2 / fulfilled 0, secessions 0, deaths 2, World Government 13429.3 at 0.08, Free Tide 163.71 at 0.05), `c5a9a0edcee8a726f6409e0176e2265b3bf498dcb44e0e4bb1975e11dc553849` (162218, news 420, contracts 2848 offered 1416 / refused 1398 / accepted 17 / breached 13 / fulfilled 4, secessions 2, deaths 1, World Government 10148.99 at 0.07, Free Tide 0 at 0.16).

  What the delta is. The streak store does not move tick 72. It moves tick 1200, and the stripped hashes above are that move. The accept-walk does not move 1847 at tick 72, because that seed offers nothing. It moves 2718 at tick 72 from the reference columns (8580 events, 27 contracts, Free Tide 1401.19) to 8564 events, 15 contracts, and Free Tide 1401.41. It moves 4096's contract counts as well. The 1200-tick pair above is the walk against the same follower filter.

  What is still not the reference pin. 1847 at tick 72 matches the reference event count (8496), news count (87), treasuries (17904.84 and 1195.88), and rates (0.14 and 0.08), and it has no streak fields, no contracts, and no deaths. The hash is `6efa334f`, not `1f350990`. 2718 at tick 72 with the walk off matches the reference event count, news count, contract breakdown, treasuries, and rates. The hash is `55080519`, not `9369a835`. The residual is inside the news records or the silent stipend purses. It is not the streak and it is not the amendment. 4096 diverges earlier, in the event count and the treasuries. Seats stay off the hash. They are a per-world WeakMap, not a process-global map, so one seed cannot leak a seat into another.

  Choices where the note left a gap. The seat map is per world. The levy snapshot is after desertion, the seat sync, and the stipend. News records are on the hashed world. A public tax item has `aboutSettlementId` null. Followers are faction mates, not every scarred captain on the dock. The autonomous declarer is the governor. `autonomousSecessionReady` remains the old owner helper and `runTick` does not call it. The offer runs before decisions, so a carrier can sail before the next tick scores. An open pair is skipped and the next accepting carrier is offered. The buyer is not removed from the carrier walk. Death compares `start * 180 + tick` with `120 * 180` as integers. Ages are not stored. Succession is vacant. A holder's death clears the acting commander and does not promote the cover. `news-posted` is omitted from the dashboard feed. The offer id is `supply-${settlementId}-${tick}`.

  A live session on the dashboard, seed 1847, no command, is not the headless pin after tick 72. Through tick 72 it matches the headless treasury: World Government 17904.84 at 0.14, `eventSequence` 8496, no contract. The plan is [batch2-001](docs/playtests/batch2-001.md).

  Choices. A debt whose creditor is null is skipped and does not block a later payable debt. `wage` is not stored on `debt-repaid`. The short streak and the side RNG are process-local maps, not world fields, so they are absent from the hash. The fork is rebuilt from `createPrototypeWorld(seed).rngState` on the first roll and is not written back. A stay emits nothing, so a restored process cannot replay a consumed stay. The 72-tick recovery never rolls. Desertion does not clear `actingCommanderId` or a governor seat. The morning's short set is taken before any desertion that morning, and characters are walked in id order. The roll is compared raw, under 0.5, and the stored value is `round(roll, 3)`. The gate draws only when the streak becomes exactly 12 and loyalty is under 0.75. The public debt sentence is `met a debt.`, which does not contain `paid`. The 0.5 is on the visible upkeep line and on the co-located `payNote`, not on the withheld public upkeep sentence.

  Dashboard HTTP on `127.0.0.1:4317`, seed 1847, `prototype-player`, live session, no command. At tick 114 Mina Vale is at Crown Harbor, morale 57.6, money 35.17, and her pay note says pay is short and morale loses 0.5. Mara's pay note is null. Sequence 13380 reads `Mina Vale's upkeep was recorded.` and does not contain `0.5`. The advance that arrives at tick 174 has no desertion, and Finn Frost and Bram Tern are still `free-tide`. The next tick's response has `Finn Frost left Free Tide Compact. The roll was 0.245.` and `Bram Tern left Free Tide Compact. The roll was 0.034.`, both with `data` null. The following read has `factionId` null on both cards. Advance then stops at tick 317 because she was captured at tick 316, and at tick 401 because she was released at tick 400. The first `debt-repaid` she can read is tick 810, sequence 107676: `Corin Hale met a debt.`, `data` null. World Government's ledger is empty and Free Tide's is null. Esme Dusk's live-session desertion is tick 630, roll 0.407, later than the headless tick 390, because the capture changed the tape. The plan is [batch1-wage-morale-001](docs/playtests/batch1-wage-morale-001.md). A blind operator ran that plan on `0f8cfac5d31807188c234af23ad8223b66f47b4c`, Node v24.21.0, ICU 78.3, one process, seed 1847, `prototype-player`, no command. Every named checkpoint matched. Verdict: `PROMOTE`.
- **Left open** — A separate operator has not run the blind plan. The side stream does not survive a process restart after a stay has consumed a roll. Desertion does not clear a governor seat. The docs commit `e4db342` is not an ancestor of this branch and was not merged.

  Choices. The sentence is appended to the existing feed row. It is not a stored event and it is not copied into the check-in briefing. The whole number is the floor of the stored absolute. The previous skill is the 3-decimal value `practicedSkill` maps to that absolute; preimages that disagree produce no line, and on 0 through 100 they do not disagree. Visibility is the card's `capabilityExact` at read time, not payload visibility and not captive-intel leadership alone. Two crossings are two sentences, leadership then trade on `worked`, strategy then leadership on `battle-resolved`. Troop quality uses the setup bands on the rounded experience: under 0.35 `ordinary`, from 0.35 up to 0.55 `gap`, from 0.55 up to 0.8 `veteran`, at 0.8 `past`. That band is not the stored promotion label. Feed counts are lifetime `worked`, `travel-started`, and `battle-resolved` from the log, null when the caller did not pass the log, and present for every character because those rows are already public. Battle totals are this character's stored victories and defeats. A world-level attacker and defender census stays off the player view.
- **Left open** — Troop experience, the promotion label, and the rarity mark are still not in the tree. A separate operator has not run the blind plan.
- **Links** — [progression](docs/design/progression.md), [progression-readout-001](docs/playtests/progression-readout-001.md) (`PROMOTE` at `fbb2eb1`)

### 2026-10-13 — The skill step writes the new absolute on the feeding event

- **Agent:** Open Era Engineer (Cursor cloud agent) | **Branch:** `feature/progression-skill-step` | **Commits:** `0372d2d` (the step and the retargeted tests). This commit records the divergence and the playtest plan. | **Type:** Behavior
- **Changed** — `practicedSkill` at `src/sim/state.ts:143` closes 0.1% of the gap to 100, `round(..., 3)`, capped at 100. `copyPracticedSkills` at `src/sim/state.ts:154` copies a numeric field and leaves a missing field. `applyEvent` calls it for `travel-started` (`src/sim/state.ts:801`, navigation), `worked` (`src/sim/state.ts:830`, leadership and trade), and `battle-resolved` (`src/sim/state.ts:942`, strategy and leadership). `practicedSkillData` at `src/sim/engine.ts:168` reads the skills before `emit`, and the three emitters spread it at `src/sim/engine.ts:1135`, `src/sim/engine.ts:1230`, `src/sim/engine.ts:2918`, and `src/sim/engine.ts:3068`. Tests: `tests/progression-skill.test.ts`. The golden fixture is unchanged. The fields are flat on `event.data` (`leadership`, `trade`, `navigation`, `strategy`). A missing number does not write. The rate is the constant 0.001. The cap is applied to the result, so a stored skill above 100 becomes 100 on the next feeding event. The two skills on one event are each taken from the pre-step value. Troop experience, discipline, the promotion label, and rarity are not written.
- **Why** — The ruling in `docs/design/progression.md` ships the skill step first. `worked`, `travel-started`, and `battle-resolved` store the new skill absolutes at rate 0.001, and `applyEvent` writes them. The 72-tick hash moves on every seed. Troop experience, the promotion label, and the rarity mark stay behind it.

### 2026-10-13 — Secession, the autonomous walker
- **Agent:** Open Era Engineer (Cursor cloud agent) | **Branch:** `feature/secession-3` | **Commits:** `a90317f` and this commit. | **Type:** Behavior
- **Changed** — The autonomous rule runs once per tick, after settlement upkeep, player commands, and this tick's tax step, before battles. Ports are walked in id order. No draw. An autonomous owner declares on the first tick all of these hold, and not again during that spell: the owner gate in section 2, and the owner is not the command holder; the holding `taxRate` is above 0.15; stability is at or below 55. The event is `secession-declared` with no `commandId` and no `player-command-resolved`. `successorId` is null, because the declarer is not the holder. `settlement.secessionDeclaredBy` records the owner for that spell and is cleared on `settlement-claimed`. It stays absent until a declaration. A human owner is not declared, in a live session or a headless run.
- **Why** — Slice 3 of [secession](docs/design/secession.md). The note says: "The autonomous rule runs once per tick, after settlement upkeep and player commands, before battles. Ports are walked in id order. No draw. An autonomous owner declares on the first tick all of these hold, and not again during that spell." The gates are the owner gate, the owner is not the command holder, the holding rate above 0.15, and stability at or below 55. Section 9 says: "Neutral on these seeds: eligible spells are 0, so the walker emits nothing. The hash moves on the first tick a holding rate above 0.15 meets stability at or below 55 with a present, free owner who is not the command holder."

### 2026-10-13 — Secession, the holder names a successor
- **Agent:** Open Era Engineer (Cursor cloud agent) | **Branch:** `feature/secession-3` | **Commits:** `9f9e655`. | **Type:** Behavior
- **Changed** — A declarer who is `commandHolderId` of the faction being left may still declare. An autonomous holder is still refused. In the same reducer step the cover-sort successor is written onto that seat: every standing order whose `issuerId` was the declarer is retargeted to `successorId`, and `commandHolderId` of the old faction resolves to that person. The successor is left out of `followerIds` and stays in the old faction, even when the follower rule would have taken them. `actingCommanderId` of the old faction is cleared, because the cover has taken the seat rather than covering a holder who has left. The new faction still mints no order, so its seat stays unnamed. `secession-declared` carries `successorId`. It is null when the declarer does not hold the seat, and those orders are not retargeted. When the cover sort is empty, the command is refused `no-successor` (`The faction would be left with nobody to take the seat.`) and the holder stays. The public sentence stays `{owner} declared {settlement} separate from {old faction}.` A viewer who may see the payload, the actor or a member of the previous faction, also hears `{successor} takes the seat.`
- **Why** — The old faction was left headless, and the declarer's existing orders made them command holder of the new faction. The aging inherit rule retargets the holder's orders onto the cover. This ruling uses that cover sort and refuses when nobody remains.
- **Verified** — Base `f85d59a`. Node v24.21.0, ICU 78.3. `npm run typecheck` is clean. **408 tests.** The 72-tick golden fixture passed inside `npm test`. No `golden:update`. Headless hashes match. 72 ticks: `4ea893a485b05ce6eab599919765903ade9a0ce45f383437f4698064faa7a297` (1847, 8413 events), `d376ad02c6e7c9b03dd0eb4db1c3c137ac7a00d61a096673e12ae4fdabbdfef2` (2718, 8456), `ac780b3999c3da53f38c0cd16301dfa7f60562a35796f5fa0451c969c9ed08de` (4096, 8261). 1200 ticks: `28866eb80bd48798c78791dd8f0c4093b9ffecfb8009421db7dd2c109d4f1e47` (1847, 161658 events, captures 16, releases 16, records 12), `712b60edc75c313f17e774951f43aa84fcd487fbc29c422215cd602b19665be8` (2718, 161613, captures 14, releases 13, records 10), `b0ac72fe452697010e58570136737e4dd8ed12770a2e86863bfdaa76ee07e114` (4096, 162327, captures 9, releases 9, records 7). Zero `secession-declared` on those runs. `./scripts/evaluate-milestone.sh secession-3` is not run yet.
- **Left open** — Slice 3, the autonomous walker. The cover sort does not prefer a seated acting commander over a higher score. A captive is not a successor. Order expiry after this step can still leave the seat unnamed.

### 2026-10-13 — Secession, the declaration
- **Agent:** Open Era Engineer (Cursor cloud agent) | **Branch:** `feature/secession-2` | **Commits:** `2f4bd73`. | **Type:** Behavior
- **Changed** — `declare-secession` is a player command. The body is `playerId`, `type`, `settlementId`, and an optional `name`. Omitted, the faction name is the owner's. The boundary refuses when the port is unknown, the name is present and empty, the port has no faction, the character is not the owner, the owner is not a member of the holding faction, the owner is captive, at sea, or away, the owner is an autonomous command holder, or a secession is already queued for that player or that port. A player who holds the seat may declare. The tax and stability gate is not required. `processPlayerCommands` runs after settlement upkeep. A refusal emits `player-command-failed`. A success emits `secession-declared` and then `player-command-resolved`. The event's `actorId` is the owner, `targetId` is the previous faction, and `settlementId` is the port. `data` carries `commandId`, the previous faction, the owner, sorted `followerIds`, `garrisonFollowed`, the garrison absolute, stability, and the new faction (`id` `secession-{owner}-{port}-{tick}`, `name`, `color` `#2f6f4e`, `treasury` 0, `taxRate` 0.08). The acting commander is replaced from the cover sort when that id is leaving, and otherwise stays. The reducer writes those absolutes and does not re-test loyalty. Population, the owner's purse, and the old treasury stay. World `version` stays 5. The feed sentence is `{owner} declared {settlement} separate from {old faction}.` `secession-declared` is on `summaryStaysWhenWithheld`. The payload stays visible to the actor and to a member of the previous faction. The queued line is `Command queued for {actor}: declare {place} separate` and adds ` as {name}` when the command passed one. `capabilities.commandTypes` and `capabilities.requests.commands.body` publish the command. There is no autonomous caller.
- **Why** — Slice 2 of [secession](docs/design/secession.md). The readout was slice 1. This slice is the act.

- **Changed** — `secessionReadout` is computed when the settlement panel is built. It reports `ownerId`, whether the section 2 owner gate passes, how many anchored members would follow, and whether the garrison would follow. `taxOpen` repeats the follower count and the garrison flag with the tax line treated as open. The sum is `personality.loyalty + (loyaltyAdjustment ?? 0)`. A follower must be under 0.75, a member of the holding faction, anchored, not the owner, and not `commandHolderId`. Nothing is written onto the world or an event. There is no `declare-secession` command.

### 2026-10-13 — Leader tax, graded stability drift
- **Agent:** Open Era Engineer (Cursor cloud agent) | **Branch:** `feature/leader-tax-stability` | **Commits:** `31bc37d` (the drift and the band-edge tests). This commit adds the unread plan and this entry. | **Type:** Behavior
- **Changed** — `produceSettlements` subtracts `taxDrift` on the stability write it already rounds. The holding rate is the faction that holds the port on that tick. A neutral port adds zero. An active battle still skips the settlement. Above 0.15, `bands = ceil((nearest hundredths - 15) / 5)` and the drift is `bands * 0.03`. Rates 0.16 through 0.20 subtract 0.03, 0.21 through 0.25 subtract 0.06, and 0.26 through 0.30 subtract 0.09. A rate at or below 0.15 subtracts zero. There is no recovery for a low rate. The expression is `stability - shortage * 0.35 + (shortage === 0 ? 0.03 : 0) - taxDrift`, then the same clamp to 0–100 and the same 3 digits. The set still resolves after upkeep, so the new rate starts on the next peaceful upkeep. No autonomous writer.
- **Why** — The Lead ruled the drift graded. Scenario rates 0.14 and 0.08 sit at or below 0.15, so a headless run must stay on the existing hashes. Slice 3, the autonomous choice, stays out.

### 2026-10-13 — Leader tax, the free holder's rate
- **Agent:** Open Era Engineer (Cursor cloud agent) | **Branch:** `feature/leader-tax` | **Commits:** `deae157` (the command, the reducer, and the tests). This commit adds the unread plan and this entry. | **Type:** Behavior
- **Changed** — `set-tax-rate` is a player command. The body is `playerId`, `type`, and an absolute `taxRate`. The faction is the player's. Only the free command holder is accepted. The next tick emits `tax-rate-set` with `factionId`, `taxRate`, `previousTaxRate`, and `taxRateSetTick`. ### 2026-10-01 — Treasury wording

No pronoun field was added. A recruit the reader can see, or an own-faction recruit that already shows a treasury draw, is one sentence: `Mara Vane recruited 8 at Crown Harbor for 96 from the purse.`, `Mara Vane recruited 8 at Crown Harbor for 96 from the treasury.`, or `Sable Morrow recruited 8 at Glassport for 96: 18 from the treasury and 78 from the purse.` That recruit sentence replaces the draw sentence for those rows. ### 2026-10-01 — Player purse source and allowance wording
- **Agent:** Open Era Engineer (Cursor cloud agent) | **Branch:** `feature/treasury-source` | **Commits:** `ffbb3ef` (the command field), `2c6915d` (the sentences). This commit records the gate and the unread plan. | **Type:** feature
- **Changed** — `character-action` and `offer-contract` take optional `source`. `"purse"` pays the purse and does not draw the allowance or the treasury. Omitted, a member still draws the allowance then the purse, and the free holder's accept event echoes `"treasury"`. Any other value, and `source` on any other command type, is `invalid-source` with `source is "purse" or omitted; … was requested`. A captive still gets `character-captive`. A short purse still gets `insufficient-money` or `insufficient-passage`. The field is on `capabilities.requests`. A purse voyage stores `source: "purse"` on that travel only, so later sea ticks skip the allowance. Headless travel does not grow the field. The draw sentence adds `and paid {purseDrawn} from their purse` when the event already has a purse share above 0, and stays `{name} drew {amount} from the treasury.` when the purse paid nothing. Characters have no pronoun, so the clause uses `their`. An omitted remainder on an own-faction capped officer reads `none spent today`. The free holder's cap reads `no cap`. Rival rows stay null. A hidden treasury still reads `not visible to you`.

- **Changed** — Recruit, passage, provision buys, goods buys, and contract escrow draw the faction treasury's daily allowance first (18, which is `ticksPerDay` 6 times the passage charge of 3), then the purse.   | 2718 after | 10 | 8 | 7 | 8, 741.80 | 3, 1230.20 | 370.92 | 370.88 | 42097 / 14972.74 |
  | 4096 before | 6 | 6 | 6 | 5, 530.71 | 2, 598.41 | 0 | 0 | 45937.12 / 12940.52 |
  | 4096 after | 16 | 12 | 8 | 12, 854.67 | 8, 1589.64 | 427.37 | 427.30 | 45522.66 / 14512.02 |

  The shares sum to the ransom total on every seed. The treasury side is ahead by the odd cents. On 1847 and 4096 the first leader is Pax Ash. His purse is read by recruit, travel, and trade, so the next decision can change and the later captures and releases are a different campaign. On 2718 the first leader is Mara Vane. She does not choose autonomous actions, and the treasury is not an input to the planner, so the capture list stays the same until Pax is paid at tick 955. After that, Bram's payment, Iris's debt, and Mara's own later ransom change, two captures at ticks 1166 and 1168 are still open at tick 1200, and the event count falls by 6. The debt formula is unchanged. Debt counts move because the later releases are different. One 1847 release pays 0 (Esme Dusk, tick 1015), which is why 16 releases produce 15 ransoms. Blind playtest [ransom-split-001](docs/playtests/ransom-split-001.md), head `97ba340c75dc263b71b2a24f57b2f32b21abc1ea`, Node v24.21.0, agent Ransom split blind playtest: ransom-split-001 (`bc-552d0d38-8bb7-58ab-88b6-46b3cae7f427`): **PROMOTE**. Sequence 13680 and the briefing sentence are the Sable Morrow line (13.4 paid, debt 103.21, Loyalty fell, 6.7 to the Free Tide Compact treasury and 6.7 to Pax Ash). Sequence 118405 and the briefing sentence are the Dax Pike line (62.69 paid, debt 380.67, Loyalty fell, 31.35 to the World Government treasury and 31.34 to Mara Vane). Both titles are `A captain was released`. At state tick 276 Dax Pike is at Glassport with `captivity` null and `money` null, Mara's purse is 108, and the World Government treasury is 27132.81. At state tick 902 Mara's purse is 504.36 and the World Government treasury is 40003.64. At state tick 901, before that release, `captivity` was null while `captiveIntel` showed him held at Glassport.
- **Left open** — The blind session is not run. `docs/design/captivity-debts.md` still says the release-day coins do not enter a treasury. No seeded release has a null captor faction.

### 2026-09-29 — M34: Capture wording
- **Agent:** Open Era Engineer (Cursor cloud agent) | **Branch:** `feature/capture-wording` | **Commits:** `65b55a1`, `3cbdb89`, `0fa3429`, `063aaff`, `dc85bb8`, and the commit that adds this entry | **Type:** feature
- **Changed** — Sentences are built when the state is read. A capture names the captor faction. An `outscore-loss` says the prisoner was taken on the dock after the other side won on a higher score, and the card adds `causeLabel` beside the stored cause. A battle the payload rules out of a standing win says `on a higher score`. `seatSummary` names the cover. The escape card says escape always works and names `displayedRisk`. A release with debt says loyalty fell, and the holder's release says she holds the seat again. Her own card's `loyaltyNote` names the unrounded sum the seat reads. `player.displayName` is the character's name. `skillsNote` explains a withheld leadership, and it stays null when `captiveIntel` already shows that leadership. A sea row carries `summary`, the briefing lists it, and `outOfStretch` names a ship on the same leg with no troop count. A docked card says the hold was learned. Passage is read off the upkeep row. The sea inspector label is the row's kind. `attentionCount` stays the decision count. `shownCount` and `attentionLabel` name the background lines. The merge keeps M33: `captiveIntelFor`, `writeReleaseSighting` inside the `captivity-released` reducer, and `reportedAge` from the tick the player is shown. `src/sim` changes on this branch are those M33 writes plus the chronicle lines in `reports.ts`. Questions 91–94 use the note's defaults: the view name is Mara Vane and the stored string stays Prototype Commander; the player does not see "outscore"; a ship outside her stretch is named with no troop count; the sea count is the row's kind and the port record stays Sighted troops.
- **Why** — Questions 91–94 were accepted as M34. The playtests could not tell who took the prisoner, what outscore meant, who covered the seat, or which ship was outside her stretch. The branch had been cut from `149d942`, so the merge puts those sentences on M33's captor row and release record.
- **Verified** — `npm run typecheck` clean. **273 tests.** `./scripts/evaluate-milestone.sh capture-wording` passed. Split recovery replayed 572 events. No `golden:update`. Sequence 8402, seed 2718 state tick 72, reads `World Government took Mina Vale at Crown Harbor after failed retreat` beside `captiveIntel` troops 12. Sequence 18482, event tick 155, reads `Mina Vale was released from Crown Harbor: 58.13 paid and 0 recorded as debt`. - **Agent:** Open Era Engineer (Cursor cloud agent) | **Branch:** `feature/captive-intelligence` | **Commits:** `8620666`, `34d8de9`, `c95b90b`, `f2f6d51`, `54fcd1e`, `1bdfce1`, and this commit | **Type:** feature
- **Changed** — `captiveIntelFor` in `src/dashboard/visibility.ts` derives a row while `captivity` is set and `captorFactionId` is the reader's faction. The reader does not have to be in the port. The row carries leadership, `scatteredTroops.count`, the party power that count gives, `observedTick` from the capture, confidence 1, source `direct`, and the prisoner's own-faction port beliefs (`direct` or `faction-report` only). It is on that prisoner as `captiveIntel`. Live `troops` stay 0 and live `partyPower` stays 0. The row is not copied into them, and it is not stored. `writeReleaseSighting` in `src/sim/state.ts` stores `releaseSighting` inside `applyEvent` for `captivity-released`, while `captivity` is still set. Upkeep has already run and the character walk has not. The pre-event write in `processCaptivityDeadlines` is gone. The event gains no field. `reportedAge` in `src/dashboard/view-model.ts` ages a shown garrison from the floored tick, so `ageTicks` equals the world tick minus the shown tick. `combatForecast` still ages its band from the raw tick. A later release replaces it. Nothing deletes it. On anyone else's projection it is null. No new event and no rng draw. `WorldState.version` stays 5. A stale port belief, and a release record at least 18 ticks old, fire the existing "Intelligence is stale" warning. The person fields do not.

## Entries

### 2026-09-29 — M29.1: Spend the outscore roll on the dock
- **Agent:** Open Era Engineer (Cursor cloud agent) | **Branch:** `feature/outscore-dock-capture` | **Commits:** `aaf2c21`, `da4be06`, `503a384` merges `origin/main` at `557ed84`. This entry is the record commit. | **Type:** Behavior
- **Changed** — On an outscore win, the roll `completeMajorBattle` already draws is spent at `captureChanceForRisk`. The prisoner is the losing faction's member on the port, free, not travelling, and not the attacker, highest leadership plus loyalty times 50, lower id on a tie. The captor is the attacker's faction. The cause is `outscore-loss`. An empty dock spends the roll and takes nobody. No second draw. The victory, the surrender offer, and the garrison are written before that roll. A captured seat holder still runs the existing cover. Open items gained three readability rows under the battle-feed rows.
- **Why** — M29 drew that roll and discarded it, so the senior officer standing on the lost port was never the prisoner, and the command seat had nothing to cover on a quiet run.
- **Verified** — Node v24.21.0. `npm run typecheck` clean. **223 tests**. `./scripts/evaluate-milestone.sh outscore-dock-capture` passed after the merge of `origin/main` at `557ed84`. Tick-72 hashes stayed `cb04ba5d392d8b1c868cc97e54cb21b21ec171edd546bb70d0d7aba86cc69c11` (1847, 8301), `bd7d8cc44d5fa21022ecb8f8086e13dfbb9475eb025b5ae53f87e2991f90035c` (2718, 8513), `20975bf480e5aa11eeafe1ce39c36cf5ba0fa8e2d5de2bb5887a35d7b3aecc9f` (4096, 8031). Split recovery replayed 572 events. No `golden:update`. Against `e68281b`, 1200 quiet ticks: captures 12 / 8 / 6 (outscore-loss 11 / 5 / 2, the rest the old holds). Covers written: Jun Marrow at 594, Dax Pike at 957 and 1058, Ada Sorn at 1034, and none on 4096. First divergent event is `character-captured` `outscore-loss` at event tick 498 / 871 / 475 (Jun Marrow, Rook Tern, Rook Tern). The dashboard path in [outscore-dock-capture-001](docs/playtests/outscore-dock-capture-001.md) was read on this tree: four advances to state tick 476, sequence 59637 withheld, briefing `Rook Tern was captured at Glassport after outscore loss`. The blind session is not run.
- **Left open** — The blind session. The three new readability rows (the cover is unnamed, the escape line disagrees with `displayedRisk` `low`, `player.displayName` is `Prototype Commander`). Owner question 81 still says `Default: yes` rather than built. The day's-wage collector stays parked.
- **Links** — [captures under M29](docs/design/captures-under-m29.md), [outscore-dock-capture-001](docs/playtests/outscore-dock-capture-001.md), [owner questions](docs/design/owner-questions.md) 81–86

Split recovery replayed 572 events. No `golden:update`. ### 2026-09-29 — Player buy-provisions quotes its cost and stops at the depth cap
- **Agent:** Cursor | **Branch:** `feature/buy-provisions-player-fixes` | **Commits:** `12a7c66`. `8f5b97e` merges `origin/main` at `2eef285` (the captive-intelligence note). | **Type:** Behavior
- **Changed** — The player's `buy-provisions` quotes the unit price and the total on the accepted command and on the resolution events. A top-up past `marketDepth` (28.8 provisions) is filled only up to that share. The accept sets `capped: true` and the queued line names the ceiling. The purse refusal names that clamped bill. A shelf under 1 names the stock. Alongside, the low-food line names the one-order amount when the gap is past the depth. Autonomous `buy-provisions` is the old uncapped fill, and it still scores −1000 under a shelf of 1.
- **Why** — The one-click top-up could spend a whole purse and called that spend "2 money". Refusing past the depth then left an empty hold with no successful click. The share-of-stock cap already bound `buy-resource` and `trade-local`. The merged provisioning note leaves captains uncapped, because the buys that empty the small ports are already under 28.8.
- **Verified** — `npm run typecheck` clean. **213 tests**. `./scripts/evaluate-milestone.sh buy-provisions-player` passed after the clamp. Tick-72 hashes stayed `cb04ba5d…` / `bd7d8cc4…` / `20975bf4…` (8301, 8513, 8031). Split recovery replayed 572 events. No `golden:update`. `origin/main` was still `2eef285`.
- **Left open** — Away from a market, the low-food line can still say a full top-up would buy the resupply target when that gap is past 28.8. The blind session is [buy-provisions-001](docs/playtests/buy-provisions-001.md). Not-in-port text is still the shared traveling and no-location sentences.

### 2026-09-29 — M29: a higher score wins a finished major battle
- **Agent:** Open Era Engineer (Cursor cloud agent) | **Branch:** `feature/outscore-rule` | **Commits:** `713a0ad` (the rule and the tests), `00eba2e` (the flip census and Crown Harbor churn), `f29c6ea` (the playtest plan). `e0e0e21` merges `origin/main` at `f86ad31`. This entry is the record commit. | **Type:** Behavior
- **Changed** — `resolveBattlePhase` in `src/sim/engine.ts`. The end test is unchanged: phases done, troops under 8, garrison 0, health at most 15, or morale at most 12. A finished major is an attacker victory when the garrison is 0, or when troops are at least 8, health is above 15, and either morale is above 12 with the phase tally, or the attacker's last-phase score is higher while the garrison is still standing. A tie does not qualify. Surrender is the existing rule, garrison at most 15 and stability at or below `surrenderStabilityLimit`. `completeMajorBattle` takes `consumeIgnoredCaptureRoll`. That flag is set only when the higher score is what turns a standing defeat into a victory, and the function then calls `rng.next()` and ignores it. A major the old rule already called an attacker victory does not draw. An immediate battle stays `attackerScore > defenderScore`. Morale 0 does not give it another rule. No new event type. `buy-provisions` still scores −1000 when the shelf is under 1. Autonomous buying is unchanged, and `marketDepth` still does not cap it. `docs/design/world-simulation.md` and the design README say the same rule. `npm run golden:update` was not run.
- **Why** — a major that ended at morale 12 or under was recorded as the town's victory even when the attacker's score was higher. Surrender was never offered, and the captain could be captured from a fight they had outscored.
- **Verified** — baseline on `ef81bf3`, which already has M28. Node v24.21.0, ICU 78.3. `npm ci`, then `npm run typecheck` clean, **209 tests**. Nine new tests in `tests/combat.test.ts`: "a major that ends at morale 12 with a higher attacker score is an attacker victory and can offer surrender", "the same major with a lower attacker score stays a defender victory and offers no surrender", "an outscore victory does not offer surrender when stability or garrison fails the existing limit", "troops under 8 or health at 15 keep a higher-score major as a defender victory", "morale 13 does not end the major, so outscore has nothing to resolve yet", "a higher last-phase score wins a finished major that is still above morale 12", "a flipped outscore victory consumes the defeat path's capture roll and no roll is added to a win the old rule already had", "an immediate battle is still the higher score when morale is 0", "buy-provisions stays scored at -1000 when the shelf is under 1". `./scripts/evaluate-milestone.sh outscore-rule` passes after the merge: hashes `cb04ba5d392d8b1c868cc97e54cb21b21ec171edd546bb70d0d7aba86cc69c11` (1847, 8301 events), `bd7d8cc44d5fa21022ecb8f8086e13dfbb9475eb025b5ae53f87e2991f90035c` (2718, 8513), `20975bf480e5aa11eeafe1ce39c36cf5ba0fa8e2d5de2bb5887a35d7b3aecc9f` (4096, 8031). Split recovery replayed 572 events. The fixture file was not edited. The first baseline upset is event tick 498 / 517 / 475, all after tick 72. Over 1200 ticks the note's upset census (major, `defender-victory`, attacker score higher) is 21 / 16 / 13, and 0 / 0 / 0 with the rule on. The Lead's 8 / 5 / 8 is that census from the note, taken while the raid gate stayed at 15. M28's landless floor of 8 is what multiplies the morale-ended majors on this tree. Same-score fights that still occur after the rule: 3 on 1847 (Glassport, tick 498, Corin Hale, Dax Pike, Mara Calder), 1 on 2718 (Pax Ash, Cinder Key, tick 517), 1 on 4096 (Pax Ash, Glassport, tick 475). Typecheck, the 209 tests, and the gate were run again on that merge and passed with the same hashes, the same counts, and recovery still 572. No `golden:update`. The playtest plan was checked against a headless projection of the dashboard API and was not run as a session. An `awaiting-confirmation` order waits at least one tick. A free autonomous issuer (`controller.kind` `autonomous`, `captivity` null) then signs it. Anyone else, including an idle human, a captive issuer, and a missing issuer, waits `ticksPerDay` (6). Both emit `standing-order-completed` with `actorId` the issuer and `reason` `issuer-judgment` or `issuer-silent`. No `commandId` and no `player-command-resolved`. A missing tie starts from trust 0.28, affinity 0.25, respect 0.28, fear 0.08, grievance 0, obligation 0. No money, cargo, or goal progress. The order row stays. No new RNG. One deviation from the note: a missing issuer's name falls back to `order.issuerId` so the sentence still has a subject. No seeded order has a missing issuer. `npm run golden:update` was run once, on purpose.
- **Why** — a completion report from an autonomous officer sat on `awaiting-confirmation` until something else expired it. An at-sea refusal also published a score of 0 that no scoring pass had produced.

### 2026-09-29 — M26: a paid provisions delivery, with escrow
- **Agent:** Open Era Engineer (Cursor cloud agent) | **Branch:** `feature/offer-contract` | **Commits:** `b209352` (awaiting-confirmation amend, issuer-scoped queue), `d20ec2d` (the offer, the escrow, the tests), `83bc3d0` (the playtest plan), `768f817` (offer-contract-001 `PROMOTE`), `8151c8e` (the at-sea travel gate), `fc9bfb5` (the queued-offer error), `55999f4` (escrow and the travel-gate note), `39694e9` (the sailed-haul plan). `e613003` merges `origin/main` at `ac8769b`. `4e12d74` merges `origin/main` at `5d39151`. This entry is the record commit. | **Type:** Behavior
- **Changed** — Two follow-ups on the order path, player commands only. `validateOrderAmendment` in `src/sim/commands.ts` accepts `awaiting-confirmation`, and `issue-order` no longer passes a separate flag, so there is one path. A major change returns the order to pending and the plan is cleared; priority or deadline alone keeps the state; identical terms stay `no-change`. The queued `issue-order` check also requires the same issuer. `offer-contract` and `cancel-contract` in `src/sim/commands.ts`, scored in `src/sim/contracts.ts` and settled in `src/sim/engine.ts`. One type, provisions, quantity 1–200, deadline 1–720, one open contract per buyer and carrier, id `${command.id}:contract`. The price leaves the offerer when the offer event is applied and sits on `contract.escrow`. Acceptance does not take it again. Fulfilment pays the carrier from it once. Refusal, cancel, and breach return it once. A score refusal and a cancel while offered write no relationship. Fulfilment writes the victory deltas on both sides. Breach and a cancel after accept write the defeat deltas. A new offer is scored on a later tick so `offered` can be seen; an amendment is scored on the tick it is written. No autonomous proposer. The dashboard projects `contracts`. Parties see price, quantity, and escrow. A faction mate sees status and destination with those three null. Anyone else gets no row, and holding the destination does not open the event payload. A carrier already at sea, or with no `locationId`, is refused in `assessSupplyContract` before any score: gate `travel`, reason `The carrier is already at sea.` `travelDuration` returns 1 when `locationId` is null, and that 1 is not the haul, so the old check never fired and the score ran with `costBasis` 0. A docked voyage that does not fit the deadline is still gate `travel`, and that path has a positive `costBasis`. `resourcePrice` clamps scarcity to at least 0.55 against a provisions base of 1.8, so a standing carrier's price is at least 0.99 and every path that scores has a positive cost basis. The refusal event carries `reason`, and the summary appends it. A second offer queued before any contract exists keeps the code `contract-already-queued` and says `An offer to this carrier is already queued`. A mutation against a contract that already exists keeps the older sentence. `npm run golden:update` was not run.
- **Why** — a standing order never names a shelf, a quantity, or a price. The port still needs grain moved by a command, and the money has to leave the offerer when the offer is made and come back or be paid exactly once.
- **Verified** — baseline on `fef137b`, before any of this, Node v24.21.0, ICU 78.3: `npm ci`, `npm run typecheck` clean, **176 tests**, `./scripts/evaluate-milestone.sh baseline-offer-contract` passes. Split recovery replayed 572 events. After the two follow-ups: **178 tests**, golden check green, hashes unchanged. After the offer and its tests: typecheck clean, **189 tests**. ### 2026-09-29 — M21: a survey records who was anchored at the port
- **Agent:** Open Era Engineer (Cursor cloud agent) | **Branch:** `feature/party-sightings` | **Commits:** `6bfdeea` (the sightings, the tests, and the playtest), `db8db8b` (foreign tax), `4927ad4` (offshore faction label), `a223118` (foreign beach `ownerId`). This entry is the record commit on the same branch. | **Type:** Feature
- **Changed** — `partySightings` on the character, keyed by the subject, absent until the first sighting. It is not a field of `SettlementKnowledge`. `survey` writes one sighting per other character anchored in that port. A targeted `explore` that completes copies the same list onto the issuer only. An explore with no `targetId` writes nothing. A later sighting replaces a stored one only when its `observedTick` is greater or equal. Nothing deletes one. The list rides on those two `knowledge-updated` payloads. A daily refresh omits it, so replacing the knowledge entry does not erase it. Away, `troops` and `partyPower` stay null and `partySighting` is the record plus a projected age. Co-located troops stay live, including the faction-port clause. On anyone but the commander the map is null. A remote `combatForecast` adds the stored `partyPower` of sightings at that port and says `sighted parties at this port are N ticks old`, using the oldest age. The local forecast branch is unchanged. `WorldState.version` stays 5.
- **Why** — M18 stores a foreign port's garrison, population, and fortification. It does not store who was standing there. The same two writers already paid for are the channel.
- **Where the note was silent** — the person looking is left off the list. A captive in the port is included, at `troops.count`; `partyPower()` is stored and is 0 while captive; `captivity` is not copied. An empty list does not create the map. The merge forces confidence 1 and `travel: null` and does not keep extra fields. `partySightings` is not initialized in `normalizeWorldState`, because an empty object on every character would move the split-recovery hashes.
- **Projection ride-alongs** — all three are dashboard-only. `db8db8b`: a foreign port's tax is `settlementTaxRate` of the live holder when the commander is standing there or has no report, and of `knowledge.factionId` otherwise. Storing `taxRate` on the knowledge record was the other option, and writing it from `directObservation` would move the hashes, because the golden commander still observes. A faction's own rate is still the static scenario constant; no command changes it. `4927ad4`: offshore, the faction label is the report (`knownFactionId`). The lag after a claim is the stale report, the same reading as the garrison. Standing there uses the live faction. `a223118`: `ownerId` is the settlement's owner when the commander is standing in a foreign port, and stays null offshore.
- **Idle morale and health** — reproduced, not changed. Seed 1847, Mara Vane, 150 ticks, no commands: morale 0, health 59.663, money 108. `provisionDemand` is `round(0.12 + sailors * 0.008 + troops.count * 0.004)`. `upkeepCharacter` subtracts `shortage * 2.4` from morale (and adds 0.04 when the shortage is 0) and `shortage * 0.8` from health. Anchored upkeep does not write `characterMoney`. Passage is 3 per tick only while traveling. `runTick` skips the planner when `controller.kind === "human"`, so she does not `buy-provisions` or `work`. There is no troop wage. She starts at 36 provisions and demand 0.576, so the hold lasts 62 ticks, a partial shortage lands on tick 63, and full shortage after that pins morale at 0 on tick 132 while health keeps falling. This is the upkeep the briefing already describes. Feeding her from the purse inside that window would change cargo, money, morale, and health inside the 72-tick golden run. Left as is.
- **Verified** — baseline on `e8cc395`, before any of this, Node v24.21.0, ICU 78.3: `npm run typecheck` clean, **154 tests** pass, `./scripts/evaluate-milestone.sh baseline-party-sightings` passes. Hashes `d3b79fce02045cf312ad00d886005499fd18046c47fc0fd95c7e5f021e9bd653` (1847, 8338 events), `03d6d4bc643e8867f08cb12c7ec951f5c3105dd6e87b9608a2df34cd3dcee0cb` (2718, 8411), `a7cbf2a8d7d4d1bdeb7142e6891152942eeda3e3b4f580c4168aec42616010c6` (4096, 8298). Split recovery replayed 571 events to the 1847 hash. After `6bfdeea`: **159 tests** pass. After the three projection commits: **162 tests**. `npm run golden:update` was not run. The playtest is [party-sightings-001](docs/playtests/party-sightings-001.md), verdict `PROMOTE`. Finish gate on `7bfc54d`, Node v24.21.0, ICU 78.3: `npm run typecheck` clean, **162 tests** pass, `./scripts/evaluate-milestone.sh party-sightings` exits 0. Hashes `d3b79fce02045cf312ad00d886005499fd18046c47fc0fd95c7e5f021e9bd653` (1847, 8338 events), `03d6d4bc643e8867f08cb12c7ec951f5c3105dd6e87b9608a2df34cd3dcee0cb` (2718, 8411), `a7cbf2a8d7d4d1bdeb7142e6891152942eeda3e3b4f580c4168aec42616010c6` (4096, 8298). Split recovery replayed 571 events to the 1847 hash.
- **Playtest** — seed 1847, Mara Vane, dashboard HTTP, ticks 0–20. She ordered Niko Wren, already at Cinder Key. Tick 1 carried six sightings, source `faction-report`, `observedTick` 0. Niko was not on the list. Rook Tern was already sailing, live troops null, sighted troops still 26. At tick 2 Toma Reef had left, still 42. At tick 20 every stored count and every `observedTick` matched the first read. Five characters then at Cinder Key had no sighting. Verdant Cay never gained one. End hash `f334f1a32fc68767b4ea0bf9e40ab664fbe824f70d67d1df6b147ee98f64c8aa`, 2310 events.
- **Left open** — sea sightings, a passive glance, informants, captives as a channel, and a faction total. The planner does not read sightings. `locationId` and `travel` stay public on every tier, and the faction-port clause still shows live troops at a distance. An autonomous party still sails when the purse cannot cover the passage. An idle human still starves in place with money left.

### 2026-09-29 — M20: a fed port regains one soldier on its population interval
- **Agent:** Open Era Engineer (Cursor cloud agent) | **Branch:** `feature/garrison-regrowth` | **Commits:** `4ad3867` (the rule, the tests, and the golden fixture). This entry, the playtest, and the design status are the record commit on the same branch. | **Type:** Balance
- **Changed** — peacetime upkeep, and nothing around the raid gate or the surrender line. In `produceSettlements`, a settlement that is not in battle gains one garrison when `shortage === 0` and garrison is under `round(population / 70)`, on a world tick that is a positive multiple of `max(6, round(200000 / population))`. Both rounds are to the nearest integer. The soldier is added to the garrison already carried by `settlement-upkeep`. Shortage still only reduces garrison. No new event, no `garrisonGain` field, and no RNG draw.
- **Why** — M19 lets a battered port change hands and then sit at 6–13. The raid gate is still garrison 15, so the new owner is safe for the rest of the run. Fast population regrowth is the measured line that makes the first claim raidable again in 54 to 172 ticks, keeps Crown Harbor with World Government, and leaves both factions above their opening power.
- **Where the note was silent** — every settlement counts, including neutral Verdant Cay. The recommendation's table already regrows it from 70 toward 84; a faction treasury is not required. The interval is the world clock, not ticks since the claim and not a counter of fed ticks. Tick 0 is the opening figure and is not a gain; counting it would put Verdant Cay at 85 by tick 400, and the table says 84. A shortage, the ceiling, or a battle that skips the settlement drops that soldier. It is not made up on the next tick. `round` here is the nearest integer, not the 3-decimal `round` used for stocks.
- **Verified** — baseline on `0768d06`, before any of this, Node v24.21.0, ICU 78.3: `npm run typecheck` clean, **150 tests** pass, `./scripts/evaluate-milestone.sh baseline-garrison-regrowth` passes. Hashes `0720557dd12bfc1e52ed9bf58386d4501bcbcc5dddccd4fbad369bd512afa53f` (1847, 8338 events), `bd02bf90bcbf4757f9d1a895b8d9622c0e3f9c0d35bec5ebc4cfd5f1bae4edd7` (2718, 8417), `b940a89a2d46985fa92cdcd4203e54a35af71c3f60ea95164ce1a01476db23aa` (4096, 8298). Split recovery replayed 571 events to the 1847 hash. After `4ad3867`: typecheck clean, **154 tests** pass. `npm run golden:update` on the same Node and ICU. New hashes `d3b79fce02045cf312ad00d886005499fd18046c47fc0fd95c7e5f021e9bd653` (1847, **8338** events), `03d6d4bc643e8867f08cb12c7ec951f5c3105dd6e87b9608a2df34cd3dcee0cb` (2718, **8411**), `a7cbf2a8d7d4d1bdeb7142e6891152942eeda3e3b4f580c4168aec42616010c6` (4096, **8298**). Those counts are the note's fast table. There is no count difference to explain. Seed 2718 moves by 6 events because Cinder Key, claimed at tick 32, regrows inside the window. Seeds 1847 and 4096 keep 8338 and 8298 and still change hash, because Verdant Cay gains at ticks 28 and 56 (70 toward 84). The note did not hash the prototypes. Split recovery still replayed 571 events, to the new 1847 hash. A 400-tick headless run matches the note's fast table on claims, end garrisons, owners, and power: 1244.54 / 2960.5, 1470.13 / 2956.17, 1749.98 / 2982.44. `./scripts/evaluate-milestone.sh garrison-regrowth` passes on these hashes. The playtest is [garrison-regrowth-001](docs/playtests/garrison-regrowth-001.md), verdict `PROMOTE`.
- **Playtest** — seed 1847, Mara Vane, dashboard HTTP, ticks 0–150, no survey and no command. At tick 150, still at Crown Harbor, Cinder Key was the opening rumor of 96, age 166, ground null, and Glassport was a faction-report of 143, ground null. Crown Harbor was unowned World Government, garrison 172. The log has Cinder Key claims at ticks 70 and 139, an upkeep at garrison 15 on tick 124, and one Glassport claim at tick 98. Between the Cinder Key claims the port was her faction's, so the owned record showed the live garrison. That window closed when Free Tide took it.
- **Left open** — characters, parties, and factions still have no channel. An autonomous party still sails when the purse cannot cover the passage. While a faction holds a port, an offshore member of that faction reads the live garrison; that is the owned record, and it is how Mara saw Cinder Key at 14 and then 7. Glassport's tax moved from 0.14 to 0.08 while the faction label stayed World Government and the garrison stayed 143. Standing still emptied her hold: morale 0 and health 59.663 at tick 150, with 108 money untouched.

### 2026-09-29 — M19: a battered port can surrender without another raid
- **Agent:** Open Era Engineer (Cursor cloud agent) | **Branch:** `feature/raid-floor` | **Commits:** `504996c` (the slide, the tests, the golden fixture, and the playtest). The purse trial was reverted and is not in the history. | **Type:** Balance
- **Changed** — surrender, and nothing around it. After an attacker victory, `resolveImmediateBattle` and `completeMajorBattle` offer surrender when garrison is still at most 15 and stability is at or below `surrenderStabilityLimit`: `min(80, 30 + 10 × max(0, 15 − garrison))`. The autonomous raid gate stays `garrison >= 15`. The player raid check still has no garrison test. Peacetime upkeep is untouched. The predicate reads the garrison and stability the battle just wrote. It does not read a survey, a rumor, or a motive.
- **Why** — Glassport, and the same shape on other faction ports, was battered until raids stopped and surrender had not been offered. The blow landed under garrison 15 with stability still above 30, and stability then climbed. Slope 10, cap 80, is the measured line that gives each of those ports one owner and keeps the raid gate shut afterward.

Inside 72 ticks the claims moved: seed 1847 Glassport from tick 87 (outside the window) to tick 51, `character-18`, and Cinder Key from Niko Wren to `character-05`; seed 2718 Cinder Key from Orin Rill at 32 to `character-08` at 35, Glassport from Dax Pike at 41 to `character-19` at 48; seed 4096 gained a Cinder Key claim at tick 51 (`character-06`) that the slope-10 table has at tick 106, and Glassport's claimant moved from Pax Ash at tick 46 to `character-21` at tick 54.
